Pacific Hardware Enterprise REST API Documentation

Interactive production reference organized by User Journey (starting with Authentication). Includes JSON Request Bodies, Expected Outcomes, and Error Schemas.

⚡ Total Endpoints: 324 🌐 Base URL: /api/v1 🔒 Auth: JWT Bearer ⚡ Average TTFB: < 20ms

1. Authentication & Onboarding

User registration, OTP verification with B2C auto-login, authentication, password recovery, token rotation, and session management.

POST /api/v1/auth/register 🔒 Auth Required

Source File: auth.routes.ts

Headers: Content-Type: application/json | Authorization: Bearer <accessToken>

📥 Request Body ({ }) 
{
  "email": "customer@example.com (string, required)",
  "password": "Password123! (string, min 8 chars)",
  "firstName": "Rahul (string, required)",
  "lastName": "Sharma (string, required)",
  "phone": "+919876543210 (string, optional)",
  "companyName": "Acme Hardware Ltd (string, optional)",
  "gstin": "27AAAAA0000A1Z5 (string, 15 chars, optional)"
}
✅ Expected Outcome (201 Created) 
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "userId": "user-uuid-99",
    "email": "customer@example.com",
    "requiresVerification": true
  },
  "message": "Registration successful. Please verify your email."
}
⚠️ Possible Error Responses
[
  {
    "code": "400 Bad Request",
    "body": {
      "success": false,
      "error": {
        "code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
        "message": "Invalid parameter or schema validation failed",
        "details": [
          "email must be a valid email address"
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "code": "401 Unauthorized",
    "body": {
      "success": false,
      "error": {
        "code": "UNAUTHORIZED",
        "message": "Access token is missing or invalid"
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "code": "404 Not Found",
    "body": {
      "success": false,
      "error": {
        "code": "NOT_FOUND",
        "message": "Requested resource was not found"
      }
    }
  },
  {
    "code": "500 Internal Server Error",
    "body": {
      "success": false,
      "error": {
        "code": "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
        "message": "An unexpected server error occurred"
      }
    }
  }
]

📥 Request Body ({ }) 
{

      "id": "user-uuid-99",
      "email": "customer@example.com",
